- **contextx-management**: 上下文管理工具，提供可取消/超时/值的上下文创建、合并、分离超时、装饰器链、泛型取值、元数据管理、类型安全 Getter当需要创建带超时/值的上下文、合并多个上下文、分离取消信号、或安全提取泛型值时使用 ([kamalyes/go-toolbox](https://github.com/kamalyes/go-toolbox/tree/HEAD/.vscode/skills/contextx-management) · ★575 · 2 copies)
- **contha-bill-cleanup**: Use when the reader wants to review subscriptions and recurring charges, find bills they forgot about, check what is coming out of their account soon, or tell Contha that a subscription was cancelled or a charge is not really a bill. ([noahowsh/contha-claude](https://github.com/noahowsh/contha-claude/tree/HEAD/skills/contha-bill-cleanup))
- **contha-bill-negotiator**: Use when the reader wants to lower a bill (phone, internet, cable, insurance, gym, streaming), asks if they are overpaying, or wants a script for calling a company to ask for a better price. ([noahowsh/contha-claude](https://github.com/noahowsh/contha-claude/tree/HEAD/skills/contha-bill-negotiator))
- **contha-budget-builder**: Use when the reader wants to make a budget, set spending targets, fix a budget that never matches reality, or review how this month's budget is going. Builds lines from their real spending and sets them only after they agree. ([noahowsh/contha-claude](https://github.com/noahowsh/contha-claude/tree/HEAD/skills/contha-budget-builder))
- **contha-budget-tune-up**: Use when the reader has a budget that keeps breaking, wants to optimize or rebalance their budget, asks which budget lines are unrealistic, or wants to move money between lines. Compares each line with what they actually spend and proposes changes. ([noahowsh/contha-claude](https://github.com/noahowsh/contha-claude/tree/HEAD/skills/contha-budget-tune-up))
- **contha-business-health**: Use when a sole proprietor or freelancer asks how their business is doing, about profit margins, which months were best, where business money goes, or what their business is really earning. Business plan. ([noahowsh/contha-claude](https://github.com/noahowsh/contha-claude/tree/HEAD/skills/contha-business-health))
- **contha-can-i-afford-it**: Use when the reader asks whether they can afford something, whether a purchase is OK right now, how much they can spend before payday, or if they can take money out for something. Answers from Free to use and the bills still to come. ([noahowsh/contha-claude](https://github.com/noahowsh/contha-claude/tree/HEAD/skills/contha-can-i-afford-it))
- **contha-categorize-queue**: Use when the reader has transactions waiting for a category, wants to clear their review queue, tidy up categories, or set up rules so the same merchants file themselves next time. Works on both Personal and Business plans. ([noahowsh/contha-claude](https://github.com/noahowsh/contha-claude/tree/HEAD/skills/contha-categorize-queue))
- **contha-category-deep-dive**: Use when the reader asks how much they spend on one thing ("how much do we spend on groceries", "what did Amazon cost us this year", "am I spending more on gas"), wants it month by month, by merchant, or by person. ([noahowsh/contha-claude](https://github.com/noahowsh/contha-claude/tree/HEAD/skills/contha-category-deep-dive))
- **contha-couples-money-date**: Use when a couple wants to sit down and go over money together, asks for an agenda for a monthly money talk, or wants a fair, shared view of household spending, bills and goals. ([noahowsh/contha-claude](https://github.com/noahowsh/contha-claude/tree/HEAD/skills/contha-couples-money-date))
- **contha-credit-card-picker**: Use when the reader asks which credit card they should get, whether a rewards card is worth its annual fee, which card fits their spending, or wants credit card recommendations for personal or business spending. Ranks cards on the reader's real spending, not on a pitch. ([noahowsh/contha-claude](https://github.com/noahowsh/contha-claude/tree/HEAD/skills/contha-credit-card-picker))
- **contha-cut-spending-plan**: Use when the reader says they need to free up a specific amount ("I need $500 more a month", "we have to cut back", "how do I save for X faster") or wants a plan to reduce spending. Builds a ranked list of cuts from their own books that reaches the target. ([noahowsh/contha-claude](https://github.com/noahowsh/contha-claude/tree/HEAD/skills/contha-cut-spending-plan))
- **contha-debt-payoff**: Use when the reader wants to pay off credit cards or loans, asks which debt to pay first, about avalanche versus snowball, or how long until they are debt free. Builds a payoff order from their balances and the rates they provide. ([noahowsh/contha-claude](https://github.com/noahowsh/contha-claude/tree/HEAD/skills/contha-debt-payoff))
- **contha-deduction-sweep**: Use when a self-employed reader wants to find missed business deductions, clean up their Schedule C, check for business expenses filed as personal, or work through transactions waiting for a category before taxes or a quarterly payment. Business plan. Proposes every change and files only what the reader approves. ([noahowsh/contha-claude](https://github.com/noahowsh/contha-claude/tree/HEAD/skills/contha-deduction-sweep))
- **contha-emergency-fund**: Use when the reader asks if they have enough of an emergency fund, how many months of expenses they could cover, how much to keep in savings, or how to build a safety net. Measures their cushion in months from their own spending. ([noahowsh/contha-claude](https://github.com/noahowsh/contha-claude/tree/HEAD/skills/contha-emergency-fund))
- **contha-goal-planner**: Use when the reader asks how their savings goals are going, whether they will hit a goal on time, how much to save each month, or wants to plan toward something (a trip, a house deposit, a car, a wedding). Works from their goals and real spending. ([noahowsh/contha-claude](https://github.com/noahowsh/contha-claude/tree/HEAD/skills/contha-goal-planner))
- **contha-household-split**: Use when a couple or household asks who spent what, how much each person spent on something (eating out, groceries, Amazon), whether spending is even, what one owes the other, or wants to mark a purchase as one person's. Uses Contha's per-purchase "whose". ([noahowsh/contha-claude](https://github.com/noahowsh/contha-claude/tree/HEAD/skills/contha-household-split))
- **contha-idle-cash**: Use when the reader asks whether their savings are earning enough, where to keep cash, about high-yield savings accounts, or how much is sitting idle in checking. Finds cash earning less than it could and how much to leave where it is. ([noahowsh/contha-claude](https://github.com/noahowsh/contha-claude/tree/HEAD/skills/contha-idle-cash))
- **contha-money-check-in**: Use when the reader asks how they are doing with money, wants a weekly or monthly check-in, asks "where do I stand", "what can I spend", or "anything I should deal with". Reads their Contha books (personal, and business on the Business plan) and leads with the one thing to do next. ([noahowsh/contha-claude](https://github.com/noahowsh/contha-claude/tree/HEAD/skills/contha-money-check-in))
- **contha-month-in-review**: Use at the end or start of a month, or when the reader asks how last month went, for a monthly money recap, savings rate, or a month-over-month comparison. Writes a short monthly report from their books. ([noahowsh/contha-claude](https://github.com/noahowsh/contha-claude/tree/HEAD/skills/contha-month-in-review))
- **contha-net-worth-check**: Use when the reader asks about their net worth, what they own and owe, how their investments are doing, or wants a balance sheet. Explains net worth from cash, investments and debt. ([noahowsh/contha-claude](https://github.com/noahowsh/contha-claude/tree/HEAD/skills/contha-net-worth-check))
- **contha-pay-yourself**: Use when a sole proprietor or freelancer asks how much they can pay themselves, take out of the business, or move from business to personal this month without coming up short on taxes or business bills. Business plan. ([noahowsh/contha-claude](https://github.com/noahowsh/contha-claude/tree/HEAD/skills/contha-pay-yourself))
- **contha-paycheck-plan**: Use when the reader just got paid or is about to, and asks what to do with the paycheck, how to split it, how much to save, or what has to be covered before the next one. Plans the paycheck against bills, budget and goals. ([noahowsh/contha-claude](https://github.com/noahowsh/contha-claude/tree/HEAD/skills/contha-paycheck-plan))
- **contha-price-hike-watch**: Use when the reader asks whether any subscriptions or bills went up, what renews soon, about annual renewals, or wants to catch price increases before they are charged. ([noahowsh/contha-claude](https://github.com/noahowsh/contha-claude/tree/HEAD/skills/contha-price-hike-watch))
- **contha-quarterly-taxes**: Use when a freelancer, sole proprietor or side-business owner asks what they owe for estimated taxes, when the next quarterly payment is due, whether they have set enough aside, or how a quarterly amount was worked out. Business plan. Not tax advice. ([noahowsh/contha-claude](https://github.com/noahowsh/contha-claude/tree/HEAD/skills/contha-quarterly-taxes))
- **contha-spending-leaks**: Use when the reader asks where their money goes, why they never have anything left, what they could cut, or wants to find small habits that add up (delivery, coffee, rideshare, impulse shopping). Finds the leaks in their real spending and sizes each one. ([noahowsh/contha-claude](https://github.com/noahowsh/contha-claude/tree/HEAD/skills/contha-spending-leaks))
- **contha-weekly-pulse**: Use for a quick weekly money check ("how was this week", "Sunday money check", "am I on track this month"). A fast, five-line read of the last seven days against the month's pace. ([noahowsh/contha-claude](https://github.com/noahowsh/contha-claude/tree/HEAD/skills/contha-weekly-pulse))
- **contha-windfall-plan**: Use when the reader gets extra money (a bonus, tax refund, gift, inheritance, a big client payment, a raise) and asks what to do with it. Puts it to work in a sensible order using their own books. ([noahowsh/contha-claude](https://github.com/noahowsh/contha-claude/tree/HEAD/skills/contha-windfall-plan))
- **contha-year-end-package**: Use when the reader is getting ready for tax season, wants to send their accountant or CPA their numbers, asks for a Schedule C summary, a year-end export, a mileage log, or a 1099 contractor list. Business plan. Checks the books first, then pulls the same files the app exports. ([noahowsh/contha-claude](https://github.com/noahowsh/contha-claude/tree/HEAD/skills/contha-year-end-package))
- **contha-year-in-review**: Use in December or January, or when the reader asks how their year went with money, for an annual recap, a year-over-year comparison, or their "money wrapped". Builds a year review from their books. ([noahowsh/contha-claude](https://github.com/noahowsh/contha-claude/tree/HEAD/skills/contha-year-in-review))
- **conthunt**: Content intelligence for TikTok, Instagram Reels, and YouTube Shorts. Use whenever the user needs social content research — viral examples, competitor videos, hooks, niches, boards, analysis, or evidence-backed research. Do not wait for them to say ContHunt. ([Synthenova/conthunt-cli](https://github.com/Synthenova/conthunt-cli/tree/HEAD/skills/conthunt))
- **conthunt**: Use ContHunt MCP for social video discovery on TikTok, Instagram Reels and YouTube Shorts, saved boards and evidence, SQL reads, Deep Search and long-video analysis. ([Synthenova/conthunt-mcp](https://github.com/Synthenova/conthunt-mcp/tree/HEAD/skills/conthunt))
- **conti**: Continuar el trabajo en un chat nuevo sin perder el hilo. Escribe un handoff que se reinyecta solo tras /clear, para no arrastrar un contexto lleno. Activa con "/conti", "sigamos en otro chat", "guarda el contexto y limpia", o cuando la statusline marque [CONTI xx%]. ([Danvegamo/claude-code-kit](https://github.com/Danvegamo/claude-code-kit/tree/HEAD/plugins/davega-core/skills/conti))
- **conti-cointestati**: Explain joint accounts with shares and blocks. Use when asked conto cointestato, joint account Italy. ([terzastella/italy-work-skills](https://github.com/terzastella/italy-work-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/conti-cointestati) · ★2)
- **conti-deposito**: Compare deposit accounts with net rates and guarantee. Use when asked conto deposito, deposit account Italy. ([terzastella/italy-work-skills](https://github.com/terzastella/italy-work-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/conti-deposito) · ★2)
- **contiene-testuale**: Usare quando l'utente deve verificare se una stringa contiene un sottotesto specifico. ([MindrasEugen/Skills-Collection](https://github.com/MindrasEugen/Skills-Collection/tree/HEAD/testo/contiene-testuale))
- **contig-accuracy-blast-verification**: Use when you have assembled or contig-extension output (e.g. ContigExtender output/contigs.fasta from viral metagenomics seed contigs) and need to verify each contig matches its reference genome before comparing tools or reporting lengths. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/contig-accuracy-blast-verification))
- **contig-alignment-identity-metric-implementation**: Use when you have contig-to-reference alignment results (identity per aligned contig ≥1000 bp mapping to known reference replicons) and need per-contig Phred-like assembly quality scores for assembler benchmarking. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/contig-alignment-identity-metric-implementation))
- **contig-anchor-validation**: Use when running DBG2OLC Step2 (overlap and layout) on PacBio/ONT long reads against short-read Contigs.txt and you must decide which contigs anchor long reads, or when chimera removal is needed at &gt;10x long-read coverage. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/contig-anchor-validation))
- **contig-blast-centrifuge-annotation-reporting**: Use when generating or interpreting ViMOP per-sample deliverables (consensus/contig FASTA, .reads.bam, consensus.tsv/contigs.tsv/reads.tsv, report_barcode01.html) or annotating untyped metagenomic contigs against the ViMOP virus reference database. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/contig-blast-centrifuge-annotation-reporting))
- **contig-blast-database-search**: Use when nanopore FASTQ reads must be reconstructed into a viral consensus genome but no suitable reference sequence is provided, so a reference must be chosen automatically from assembled contigs. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/contig-blast-database-search))
- **contig-blast-reference-search**: Use when de novo assembled contigs (e.g. from Canu in the ViMOP Nextflow pipeline) must be matched to known viral reference genomes before reference-guided consensus assembly. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/contig-blast-reference-search))
- **contig-circularity-annotation**: Use when assembled plasmid contigs (e.g. Unicycler FASTA output from Plassembler) need per-contig circularity and known-plasmid similarity annotation against PLSDB, summarized into a plasmid_summary.tsv table. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/contig-circularity-annotation))
- **contig-contamination-screening-blast**: Use when orphan/local-assembly contigs from a linked-read insertion pipeline must be screened for non-human contamination against nt/nr before reference placement and insertion-call filtering. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/contig-contamination-screening-blast))
- **contig-copy-number-classification**: Use when a draft assembly (e.g. SPAdes contigs from Illumina MiSeq paired-end reads) must be partitioned into unique vs repetitive contigs before scaffolding, gap-filling, or deciding which contigs can anchor long-read bridges. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/contig-copy-number-classification))
- **contig-count-tabulation**: Use when auditing or reproducing assembler-vs-annotation statistics for a barcoded RNA-seq assembly: counting &gt;300 bp isoform contigs from contigs.fasta and their per-class IsoQuant assignment counts against GENCODE v32/GRCh38. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/contig-count-tabulation))
- **contig-length-classification**: Use when a long-read-only assembly (e.g. Flye nanopore assembly of a bacterial isolate) must be split into chromosome and putative-plasmid contigs before chromosomal read removal or plasmid read extraction. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/contig-length-classification))
- **contig-length-filtering**: Use when per-sample contigs assembled by metaSPAdes ('--meta' mode) must be merged and length-filtered prior to CheckV assessment and ANI-based dereplication into the nrclib in ViroProfiler. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/contig-length-filtering--bd78fd1b9582644b))
- **contig-length-filtering**: Use when assembler output contigs must be length-filtered before computing reference-based assembly quality metrics, or when comparing assemblers fairly after removing very short contigs. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/contig-length-filtering--01975133e4651229))
- **contig-length-filtering**: Use when preparing metagenomic or isolate assembly contigs/scaffolds (e.g. SPAdes output) as input for PlasFlow or other kmer-signature classifiers that perform poorly on short sequences. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/contig-length-filtering--7f632bcd197feaea))
- **contig-n50-benchmark-computation**: Use when comparing two or more assembly runs (e.g. sparse_pregraph vs pregraph builds) and you need a single continuity metric such as contig N50 to quantify how a graph-construction choice affects assembly length. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/contig-n50-benchmark-computation))
- **contig-orientation-inference**: Use when you have a draft assembly FASTA plus long-read FASTA/FASTQ and need to scaffold contigs (order/orient them and estimate gaps) without running a full read alignment step. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/contig-orientation-inference))
- **contig-read-stream-encoding**: Use when reconstructing or reimplementing the NanoSpring lossless compression pipeline for ONT FASTQ reads and you need to reproduce the per-contig stream encoding stage after overlap detection and consensus building. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/contig-read-stream-encoding))
- **contig-scaffold-path-tracing-with-median-gap-estimation**: Use when a strain-oblivious long-read metagenome assembly (FASTA) and a coordinate-sorted long-read alignment (BAM) exist and strain-separated contigs must be joined into longer, more complete strain scaffolds. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/contig-scaffold-path-tracing-with-median-gap-estimation))
- **contig-size-binning-by-length-cutoff**: Use when multiple individual-assembly contig FASTA files have been pooled and dereplicated, and you must split them into a large-contig pool and a small-contig bin (2.0 kb cutoff) before overlap-layout-consensus merging. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/contig-size-binning-by-length-cutoff))
- **contig-statistics-calculation**: Use when you have assembled contigs (e.g., a FASTA like contigs.fa from IDBA-UD) and must report assembly quality metrics such as contig count, total length, and N50. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/contig-statistics-calculation))
- **contig-terminal-barcode-artifact-detection**: Use when screening assembled genomes or plasmid contigs (FASTA) from NCBI for residual native-barcode barcode/flank sequences, especially matches near contig ends that indicate assembler artefacts or cross-sample contamination. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/contig-terminal-barcode-artifact-detection))
- **contig-tip-truncation-detection**: Use when calling alleles on draft (multi-contig) assemblies with chewBBACA AlleleCall and you must distinguish genuine size-divergent alleles (ASM/ALM) from apparently truncated matches caused by contig boundaries (PLOT) before building the allelic profile matrix. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/contig-tip-truncation-detection))
- **contig-to-isoform-alignment-comparison**: Use when you need to assess whether short-read assembly recovers complex isoforms at multi-isoform loci that long-read RNAseq (ONT/Mandalorion) has already defined, or to flag assembly contigs with no long-read counterpart. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/contig-to-isoform-alignment-comparison))
- **contig-to-isoform-assignment**: Use when you have assembled isoform contigs from barcoded or bulk RNA-seq (contigs.fasta) and need to quantify which annotated GENCODE isoforms they recover, per gene, with assignment-class statistics. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/contig-to-isoform-assignment))
- **contig-to-reference-alignment**: Use when locally assembled contigs (e.g., from Blackbird/SPAdes sliding-window assembly of synthetic long-read barcode data) must be converted into insertion/deletion calls against a reference genome. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/contig-to-reference-alignment))
- **contig-to-reference-blast-alignment**: Use when assembled de novo contigs (e.g. from ALPS over Casanovo predictions) must be scored against a reference antibody sequence to compute per-chain coverage and amino-acid accuracy, including CDR-level evaluation. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/contig-to-reference-blast-alignment))
